- **Daemon**: Manage the public daemon profile — a digital representation of what you're working on. DaemonAggregator reads LifeOS sources (TELOS, KNOWLEDGE, PROJECTS, MEMORY/WORK, identity) → daemon-data.json. SecurityFilter strips names/paths/credentials via deterministic patterns (NOT LLM). Workflows: UpdateDaemon, ReadDaemon, PreviewDaemon, DeployDaemon. USE WHEN daemon, update daemon, daemon profile, deploy daemon, preview daemon, read daemon, public profile, digital presence. NOT FOR LifeOS system management (use _LIFEOS). ([ZDStudios/AIOS](https://github.com/ZDStudios/AIOS/tree/HEAD/LifeOS-main/LifeOS-main/LifeOS/install/skills/Daemon) · ★7 · 3 copies)
